    '    itemsRef.current = items;\r\n' +

    '    previousQuery.current = query;\r\n' +

    '  );\r\n' +

    '  const itemsRef = useRef(items);\r\n' +
    '  useEffect(() => {\r\n' +
    '    itemsRef.current = items;\r\n' +
    '  }, [items]);\r\n' +
    '  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!initialItems);\r\n' +
    '  const [loadingMore, setLoadingMore] = useState(false);\r\n' +
    '  const [summaryLoading, setSummaryLoading] = useState(true);\r\n' +
    "  const [error, setError] = useState('');\r\n" +

    '  const moreController = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);\r\n' +
    '  const currentDay = useRef(day);\r\n' +

    "  const savedSummaries = useRef(new Map<string, SavedParameterReading['summary']>());\r\n" +

    '      void (async () => {\r\n' +

    '            if (isRosterChanged(cause) && (await recoverRoster())) return;\r\n' +
    '            setError(cause.message);\r\n' +
    '          }\r\n' +
    '        })\r\n' +
    '        .finally(() => {\r\n' +
    '          if (version === generation.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {\r\n' +

    '            setSummaryLoading(false);\r\n' +
    '          }\r\n' +
    '        });\r\n' +
    '    };\r\n' +
    '    const typing = previousQuery.current !== query;\r\n' +
    '    previousQuery.current = query;\r\n' +
    '    const timer = window.setTimeout(load, typing ? 250 : 0);\r\n' +
    '    return () => {\r\n' +
    '      window.clearTimeout(timer);\r\n' +
    '      controller.abort();\r\n' +
    '      moreController.current?.abort();\r\n' +

    '        });\r\n' +
